# Agents · N · part 3

930 entries, nextjs-ui-engineer to notebook-author. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **nextjs-ui-engineer**: Builds React Server Components, Client Components, and pages for JobbPilot's Next.js 16 App Router frontend. Uses shadcn/ui, Tailwind 4.2, and TypeScript 6 in strict mode. Triggers on new pages, components, form work, and UI implementation tasks. - **nextorm-brains**: Decision-maker and judge for nextorm — роль «решения» (слепой арбитр). Рассуждает только по evidence-файлам, собранным `nextorm-scout`, и тексту задания; по дизайну НЕ может читать продуктовый код, искать по репо или запускать команды (слепой арбитр). Выдаёт вердикты, планы, ранжирование и оценки по рубрике с явными ссылками на использованные улики. Use for any decision that must not be contaminated by raw code browsing — judging several implementations of the same feature against acceptance criteria, ranking options, choosing an approach. Triggers on "brains", "мозги", "вынеси вердикт", "суд… ([AlexeyShirshov/nextorm](https://github.com/AlexeyShirshov/nextorm/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/nextorm-brains.md) · ★4)
- **nextorm-code-auditor**: Code audit for nextorm — code smells (suppressions, IDisposable correctness, LINQ on hot paths, god classes, cache hash keys, optional "= null" parameter defaults) and public-API naming (P0/P1/P2, XML-doc coverage, surface locking). Owns docs/specs/design/code-smells-review.md and docs/specs/design/API-NAMING-REVIEW.md. Triggers on "code audit", "code smells", "API naming review", "нейминг публичного API", "XML-doc coverage", "CS1591", "подавления предупреждений", "SupressMessage", "IDisposable", "= null code smell", "optional parameter null", "lock the public API surface". May update the two… ([AlexeyShirshov/nextorm](https://github.com/AlexeyShirshov/nextorm/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/nextorm-code-auditor.md) · ★4)
- **nextorm-db-perf-analyst**: Database/ADO provider performance for nextorm — DataContext role interfaces, plan cache and Prepare(), query executor, row materialization, SQL builders, and the SqliteBenchmark* suite. Use when optimizing or measuring nextorm's database contexts (SQLite/Postgres/SQL Server/MySQL/ClickHouse). Can apply fixes. ([AlexeyShirshov/nextorm](https://github.com/AlexeyShirshov/nextorm/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/nextorm-db-perf-analyst.md) · ★4)
- **nextorm-design-engineer**: Design + performance review AND fix for nextorm — SOLID/DRY class and interface design, type design (sealed / readonly struct / Span vs Memory / ValueTask / collection return types) and the ~50 .NET performance anti-patterns. Triggers on "design review", "SOLID", "DRY", "god class", "fat interface", "type design", "sealed", "allocation anti-patterns", "refactor this", "what should we refactor next". Produces prioritized findings with file:line and then applies the selected fixes; builds and tests them. Does not benchmark. ([AlexeyShirshov/nextorm](https://github.com/AlexeyShirshov/nextorm/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/nextorm-design-engineer.md) · ★4)
- **nextorm-inmemory-perf-analyst**: In-memory provider performance for nextorm — IDataContext/InMemoryDataContext enumeration, row materializers, group-by/aggregates, set operations, and the InMemoryBenchmark* suite. Use when optimizing or measuring nextorm's in-memory context (no SQL, no database). Can apply fixes. ([AlexeyShirshov/nextorm](https://github.com/AlexeyShirshov/nextorm/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/nextorm-inmemory-perf-analyst.md) · ★4)
- **nextorm-scout**: Fact-gatherer for nextorm — роль «факты» (read-only разведка). Читает репозиторий и запускает команды, чтобы собрать СЫРЫЕ факты (file:line, вывод build/test, диффстаты, транскрипты команд) и записать их в evidence-файл. Никогда не рекомендует, не судит, не правит продуктовый код. Use when a question must be answered with facts before a decision is made, or for any read-only reconnaissance. Triggers on "scout", "собери факты", "gather evidence", "разведка", "прогони тесты и покажи вывод". ([AlexeyShirshov/nextorm](https://github.com/AlexeyShirshov/nextorm/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/nextorm-scout.md) · ★4)

- **note-writer**: Use this agent when the user invokes /jot:note to capture notes in the background without blocking their workflow. Examples: ([itsdevcoffee/devcoffee-agent-skills](https://github.com/itsdevcoffee/devcoffee-agent-skills/blob/HEAD/jot/agents/note-writer.md) · ★1)
- **note-writer**: Drafts a long-form Dexterlabs Note (build log, experiment, project writeup) from a brief. Produces a complete content/notes/&lt;slug&gt;/index.md with correct frontmatter and brand voice. Use when the user wants to write up a project, experiment, or build log. ([dxlbnl/website](https://github.com/dxlbnl/website/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-writer.md))
- **note-writer**: Writes or edits Markdown notes in the DevBook vault from instructions given by the main agent. Use when the main agent needs a new `.md` concept note created, or an existing note expanded/restructured, following the vault's conventions. Operates on `.md` files only. ([grafanaKibana/devbook.zip](https://github.com/grafanaKibana/devbook.zip/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/note-writer.md))
- **NoteAgent**: note.com記事執筆エージェント - 感情設計重視のバズるコンテンツ自動生成 ([ryoma3736/Miyabi](https://github.com/ryoma3736/Miyabi/blob/HEAD/packages/mcp-bundle/claude-plugins/miyabi-business-agents/agents/note-agent.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **notebook-analyst**: Rédige et enrichit les notebooks Jupyter d'exploration et d'évaluation. Produit des visualisations claires et des analyses commentées pour le rapport final. ([Ivan-Mustiere/Projet_MaintenancePredictive](https://github.com/Ivan-Mustiere/Projet_MaintenancePredictive/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/notebook-analyst.md))
- **notebook-analyzer**: Analyzes Jupyter notebooks to extract cell structure, data flow, imports, magics, and execution anomalies. ([pangjacque/Lumos](https://github.com/pangjacque/Lumos/blob/HEAD/agents/notebook-analyzer.md))
- **notebook-anomaly-spotter**: Fast reviewer for condensed notebook output digests (audit.md / audit.json). Flags “unexpected behavior” signals even when no exception is raised: empty results, missing expected artifacts, suspicious NaNs/zeros, path leakage, and warnings that indicate degraded correctness. Use as a downstream reviewer for notebook-runner and example-notebook-librarian. ([gpt-cmdr/ras-commander](https://github.com/gpt-cmdr/ras-commander/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/notebook-anomaly-spotter.md) · ★91)
- **notebook-anomaly-spotter**: Fast reviewer for condensed notebook output digests (audit.md / audit.json). Flags "unexpected behavior" signals even when no exception is raised: empty results, missing expected artifacts, suspicious NaNs/zeros, path leakage, and warnings that indicate degraded correctness. Use as a downstream reviewer for notebook-runner and example-notebook-librarian. ([gpt-cmdr/hms-commander](https://github.com/gpt-cmdr/hms-commander/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/notebook-anomaly-spotter.md) · ★10)
- **notebook-architect**: Frames the analysis question, picks warehouse + sample size + DataFrame engine, drafts the cell outline. Use before any notebook implementation. ([Camilool8/harness-engineering-templates](https://github.com/Camilool8/harness-engineering-templates/blob/HEAD/plugins/harness-data/agents/notebook-architect.md) · 2 copies)
- **notebook-assembler**: Compiles executed scripts into a Marimo notebook by literally copying script file contents into cells. Does not generate new analysis code, dashboards, or interactive widgets. Invoked at Stage 9 after all Stage 5-8 scripts and QA substages are complete. ([brycewang-stanford/Auto-Empirical-Research-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Auto-Empirical-Research-Skills/blob/HEAD/skills/17-DAAF-Contribution-Community-daaf/dot-claude/agents/notebook-assembler.md) · ★5.1k · 4 copies)
- **notebook-assembler**: Compiles executed scripts into a notebook (Marimo for Python, Quarto for R) by literally copying script file contents into cells. Does not generate new analysis code, dashboards, or interactive widgets. Invoked at Stage 9 after all Stage 5-8 scripts and QA substages are complete. ([DAAF-Contribution-Community/daaf](https://github.com/DAAF-Contribution-Community/daaf/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/notebook-assembler.md) · ★235)
- **notebook-author**: Drafts and executes a chapter's paired computational notebook against plan targets. Internal specialist dispatched by the bookwright writer orchestrator and the `/bookwright:notebook` command via the Agent tool; not intended for direct invocation. ([queelius/claude-anvil](https://github.com/queelius/claude-anvil/blob/HEAD/bookwright/agents/notebook-author.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
